Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the biophysics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 131. The resulting operator achieves bound O(79/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biophysics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^131 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 79-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the biophysics structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 221. Each subproblem has a solution by the bound derivation fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
